## `AFG-8730-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** sEMG-Muscle-Spasticity Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Elderly Fall Detection Millimeter-Wave Radar Sensor Calibrator
- **Intro:** sEMG-Muscle-Spasticity Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Elderly Fall Detection Millimeter-Wave Radar Sensor Calibrator — Pre-Calibration Environmental Stabilization: Ambient temperature: 23°C ± 1°C; relative humidity: 45% ± 5%; vibration amplitude: ≤ 0.1 g (peak) at 10 Hz to 500 Hz; electrode-skin i…
- **Key tolerance:** Ambient temperature: 23°C ± 1°C; relative humidity: 45% ± 5%; vibration amplitude: ≤ 0.1 g (peak) at 10 Hz to 500 Hz; electrode-skin impedance: ≤ 5 kΩ at 10 Hz.
- **Standards:** `ANSI/ISA-101.01-2015 Human-Machine Interfaces for Process Automa`, `IEC 61508:2010`, `ISO 10012:2003`, `ISO/IEC 17025:2017`
